- **eval-hillclimb**: Hill-climb a skill, prompt, or app with evals, keeping only gains beyond noise. Use for "eval this skill", "performance hill climb", "make it faster". Not for bugs, until-clean loops, or new skills. ([mchen04/my-public-ai-skills](https://github.com/mchen04/my-public-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-hillclimb))
- **eval-hillclimb**: Build a trustworthy eval for an LLM app, prompt, or pi skill inside the current repo, then hillclimb against it one change at a time with a held-out test split. Invoke explicitly with an argument, build-eval or hillclimb. Use when the user asks to create an eval, measure a prompt or skill, or improve one against an eval without overfitting. ([yasuhito/skills](https://github.com/yasuhito/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-hillclimb))
- **eval-history**: Rebuilds or updates the evolution chart of a skill or agent of this plugin (evals/history/) by replaying the current eval case on its past versions. Use for "show the evolution of &lt;skill/agent&gt;", "redo the chart", "update the history", or after an eval case was modified (outdated points). ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-history))
- **eval-hooks**: Audit Claude Code hooks defined in settings.json files for validity, performance safety, and correctness. Resolves each command against the filesystem, checks exit-code strategy for blocking hooks, flags missing timeouts, and reviews interactive vs async patterns. Use when setting up hooks for the first time, debugging a hook that never fires or hangs the agent, or doing a periodic hooks hygiene pass. ([TurtleMcTurtle/claude-code-ultimate-guide](https://github.com/TurtleMcTurtle/claude-code-ultimate-guide/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/eval-hooks) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **eval-hooks**: Audit Claude Code and Codex hooks for validity, safety, and correctness across settings.json, hooks.json, config.toml, plugin, and skill or agent frontmatter hooks. Resolves each command, checks event names, handler types, matchers, exit-code and JSON decision strategy, and timeouts against each host's documented rules, then reviews hooks one by one. Use when setting up hooks, debugging a hook that never fires, never blocks, or hangs the agent, or doing a periodic hooks hygiene pass. Not for writing a new hook from scratch. ([kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill/tree/HEAD/library/eval-hooks) · 6 copies)
- **eval-ideas**: Loop feature-interviews over a brainstorm idea set and consolidate survivors into the roadmap ([GeorgeQLe/agentic-skills](https://github.com/GeorgeQLe/agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/packs/product-design/claude/eval-ideas) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **eval-implementation**: Two-phase skill for running A/B implementation experiments. Phase 1 creates worktrees and prints launch commands. Phase 2 runs compare_implementations.py, dispatches blind graders, and prints the final report. ([brianruggieri/skills](https://github.com/brianruggieri/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff/eval-implementation))
- **eval-import**: Turn a list of questions into an eval set, whatever shape it arrived in: a JSONL a customer sent, a CSV, a spreadsheet export, a markdown doc, an email thread, or a pull from production logs. Classify each item by what came WITH the question (a query, a number, prose criteria, or nothing), write cases.jsonl and set.json per reference/case-format.md, keep the file as it arrived, and seal each question so a later edit is detectable. Never marks a value-bearing golden verified and never invents a question. Use when questions arrive from outside and need to become a set, or before the first run a… ([malloydata/publisher](https://github.com/malloydata/publisher/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-import) · ★134)
- **eval-improve**: Make the smallest safe edit that closes a diagnosed gap the model repo owns: a Malloy model edit, or a package skill under skills/. Every factual claim carries a probe receipt. Use after eval-diagnose, or when asked to fix a model so an agent can discover the right answer. Never accepts its own edit; the acceptance check belongs to eval-loop. Does not decide whether an answer was wrong (eval-answer) or why (eval-diagnose). ([malloydata/publisher](https://github.com/malloydata/publisher/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-improve) · ★134)
- **eval-improve**: Analyze eval failure diagnosis and make targeted improvements to picobot workspace files and tool implementations. Given structured diagnosis JSON with failure categories and fix targets, make the minimum edits needed to address each diagnosed issue. ([timainge/picobot](https://github.com/timainge/picobot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-improve) · ★1)
- **eval-improvement-loop**: Scans production/staging pipeline data for mistakes, builds a diagnosis report (root causes, scored solutions, eval recommendations), verifies with re-runs, and iterates prompt/code fixes against the full 6-stage eval suite until 100% pass. Use when improving pipeline quality, bootstrapping eval cases from prod anomalies, or chasing eval regressions across classification, content-gate, extraction, dedup, and enrichment. ([JoaoCarabetta/arquivo-da-violencia](https://github.com/JoaoCarabetta/arquivo-da-violencia/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/eval-improvement-loop) · ★3)
- **eval-in-data-ui**: Use the eval-in-data-ui function to evaluate Common Lisp forms against a running Data UI SBCL REPL via Slime. Only available when working in the data-ui project. ([macnod/data-ui](https://github.com/macnod/data-ui/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-in-data-ui))
- **eval-ingest**: 评测集补充：从 GitHub/本地 issue、已闭环的 sleuth trace 或本地 case 建诊断候选样本，跑 dry-run、补根因与修复、复核入池。 用户说“补评测集/补 field 样本/从 issue 加评估样本/把已闭环案例回流成样本”，或 self-evolve 的 run 显示 field 评估不足时使用。 只产 candidate，不自动 active、不改 case 知识、不自动合并；与 issue-ingest（case 沉淀）是两条出口。 ([pillumina/ascend-sleuth](https://github.com/pillumina/ascend-sleuth/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-ingest) · ★6)
- **eval-init**: 프로젝트를 분석한 뒤 대화형 인터뷰로 평가 게이트(골든셋·k·회귀 허용폭)와 품질 게이트(커버리지·검증루프 임계·CI 강도)를 확정하고, 골든셋 초안 생성 → 궤적 검사 → 승인분만 편입 → CI 배선 → baseline 기록까지 수행한다. 설치 후 `/eval`을 실제로 쓸 수 있게 만드는 1회성 셋업. - **eval-instructions**: A/B-test a change to CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, a skill, or an agent before keeping it. Use when trimming or rewriting agent instructions and you need evidence that behavior held, or to show a removed line was dead weight. ([martinemde/dotfiles](https://github.com/martinemde/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/home/dot_agents/skills/eval-instructions) · ★10)
- **eval-intake**: Guide evaluation discovery from a vague customer request, existing code or logs. Collect missing decisions progressively, preview the inputs and grading scope, and produce validated JSON/YAML. In the integrated Model Upgrade package, continue explicitly authorized evaluations through its existing AgentCore runner and decision report. ([openai-on-aws/benchmarks-openai](https://github.com/openai-on-aws/benchmarks-openai/tree/HEAD/plugins/prompt-optimizer/engine/intake/skills/eval-intake) · ★3)
- **eval-intake**: Grill the maintainer about a raw {{SUBJECT}} until it is articulated enough to research, then create the evaluation folder. Use at the intake stage of eval-run, or standalone. ([gentsk77/agency-setup](https://github.com/gentsk77/agency-setup/tree/HEAD/components/gradable-rules/templates/skills/eval-intake))
- **eval-integration**: Specialized integration evaluator for the Evaluate-Loop. Use this for evaluating tracks that integrate external services — Supabase auth/DB, Stripe payments, Gemini API, third-party APIs. Checks API contracts, auth flows, data persistence, error recovery, environment config, and end-to-end flow integrity. Dispatched by loop-execution-evaluator when track type is 'integration', 'auth', 'payments', or 'api'. Triggered by: 'evaluate integration', 'test auth flow', 'check API', 'verify payments'. ([Ibrahim-3d/orchestrator-supaconductor](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/orchestrator-supaconductor/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-integration) · ★384 · 5 copies)
- **eval-integrity**: Assess integrity of an eval, benchmark, metric, A/B test, holdout, LLM-judge, or user-study claim when the question is whether measurement, comparison, or evidence is independent and trustworthy. ([code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent](https://github.com/code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent/tree/HEAD/packages/shared-skills/skills/eval-integrity) · ★71k · 7 copies)
- **eval-integrity**: How to change, generate or read an eval in this repo without producing a number that cannot be trusted — goldens, thresholds, the quality gate, and the failure modes that make a metric lie. Use whenever touching evals/, a golden .jsonl, a threshold, or when about to quote an eval number in a decision. ([nupsea/luminary](https://github.com/nupsea/luminary/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-integrity) · ★11)
- **eval-integrity**: Audit an LLM evaluation or benchmark repo for integrity and credibility practices. Use when asked to "audit my benchmark," "is my eval trustworthy," "check my leaderboard for contamination," "review this benchmark's methodology," or "what would a reviewer attack in my eval." Greps the target repo for evidence across seven dimensions (pre-registration, contamination, holdout hygiene, judge validity, statistical honesty, reproducibility, leaderboard exclusions) and emits a scored report with file:line evidence, severity, and concrete fixes. ([conorbronsdon/agent-skills](https://github.com/conorbronsdon/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/eval-integrity) · ★10)
- **eval-integrity**: MANDATORY whenever evaluating, validating, summarizing, or reporting ANY data — eval results, metrics, gates, benchmarks, experiment or test output, training runs, measurements — and before stating ANY conclusion about what the data shows. Counters overstating results. Forces validating a metric's MEANING (not just its value), tagging every number's provenance, inspecting the ground-truth artifact, applying equal skepticism to passes and fails, and keeping claims within the evidence. Apply it on every single data evaluation, positive or negative. ([Xerialen/komodobots](https://github.com/Xerialen/komodobots/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-integrity) · ★2)
- **eval-integrity**: Read-only audit of an LLM evaluation or benchmark repo for integrity and credibility. Use when asked to "audit my benchmark," "is my eval trustworthy," "check my leaderboard for contamination," "review this benchmark's methodology," or "what would a reviewer attack in my eval." Scores seven dimensions with file:line evidence, severity, and fixes; writes a result.json. ([conorbronsdon/eval-integrity](https://github.com/conorbronsdon/eval-integrity/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **eval-internal-review**: Scores the quality of a unit's internal review round-set (G14 guidelines review + G19 security review when it fired) — reproduction rate, fix-verification-by-revert, scope coverage, severity calibration — and records later escapes (defects found after review that were in the reviewed diff). Invoke with /eval-internal-review, or via /eval-workflow. ([Haadhi76/workflow](https://github.com/Haadhi76/workflow/tree/HEAD/orchestrated-workflow/skills/eval-internal-review))
- **eval-investigation**: 「既存調査書」の妥当性と網羅性を評価します。 ([morikazu1119/Medi-Beacon-AI](https://github.com/morikazu1119/Medi-Beacon-AI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-investigation))
- **eval-is-evil-and-other-injection-basic-b26b0b80a46f602e**: A step-by-step skill for finding and removing code injection sinks: eval, exec, dynamic function construction, shell invocation, and unsafe deserialization, from a defender's review perspective. Use when an agent audits code for injection or reviews PRs that build code from strings. Triggers: 'eval is evil', 'code injection prevention', 'is eval safe', 'shell injection', 'unsafe deserialization'. Not for: SQL injection specifics, XSS specifics, or exploiting injection flaws. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-is-evil-and-other-injection-basic-b26b0b80a46f602e))
- **eval-iterate**: Advance an existing Stimulir Lab evaluation lineage by exactly one controlled iteration. Use when a user asks to improve or hill-climb an evaluated candidate. Reads only bounded typed lineage state and never consumes raw rationales, steer bodies, case text, or arbitrary server content. ([stimulir/skills](https://github.com/stimulir/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-iterate))
- **eval-iteration**: Read a skill eval result table and decide what to change next - description first when the skill never fired, re-measure both arms when with-only graders inflate scores, rerun before editing on a single judge failure, keep worked examples independent of the eval prompt, and stop after two fix rounds. Use when a skill's eval scores are given, when a score is 0 with the skill, when without and with are both 1.00, or when a worked example may mirror the eval case. Not for writing a skill from scratch or for installing a plugin. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-factory/skills/eval-iteration))
- **eval-judge**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-llm/eval-judge) · ★745)
- **eval-judge**: Decide whether ONE answer matches its golden, and say whether you believe the golden. Read this before emitting any verdict. Covers containment, column pairing, near_match, refusals, and the gold_status judgement. Use when scoring an attempt in an evaluation run; never to conduct a run (eval-loop), diagnose a failure (eval-diagnose) or edit a model (eval-improve). ([malloydata/publisher](https://github.com/malloydata/publisher/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-judge) · ★134)
- **eval-judge**: Grade one lvkit MCP eval answer against its question and expected-answer notes on three axes — did it use lvkit, is it correct, did it fabricate anything. Returns a per-axis verdict with reasons and the likely MCP fix. For open-ended eval questions the pytest harness can't assert. ([pragmatest-dev/lvkit](https://github.com/pragmatest-dev/lvkit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-judge) · ★52)
- **eval-judge**: Build a grader for an agent's or model's outputs that agrees with the user's own judgment: collect about 20 real runs, have the user label them, write a rubric, run the grader, measure agreement, fix the disagreements, repeat, and check on a held-out set. Works with any model or CLI. Use for "build an eval", "LLM as judge", "grade these outputs", "auto-score the agent", "is the new prompt better", or before tuning a prompt or skill by numbers. ([ychampion/ystack](https://github.com/ychampion/ystack/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-judge) · ★5)
- **eval-judge**: Score LLM and agent outputs using LLM-as-judge techniques — direct scoring against rubrics or pairwise comparison between two outputs. Includes built-in bias mitigation for position bias, length bias, and self-enhancement bias. Load when the user asks to score an output, judge a response, evaluate against a rubric, compare two outputs, do direct scoring, run pairwise comparison, or says "rate this", "which response is better", "score this against the rubric", "judge this output", "LLM as judge this". Sub-skill of eval-output orchestrator. ([dvy1987/agent-loom](https://github.com/dvy1987/agent-loom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-judge) · ★4)
- **eval-judge**: 判官：给一个样本上的一个 scorer 打分。一轮实验到了判官这一步、要重判某几个 scorer 或样本、要单独重判一批报告，或要结束判官会话时用。 ([guohaonan-shy/harold-skills](https://github.com/guohaonan-shy/harold-skills/tree/HEAD/ai-eval/skills/eval-judge))
- **eval-judge**: Use this when you need to grade a single open-ended output against a rubric (LLM-as-judge) — to get a PASS/FAIL with a score, deterministically offline and with a real model when live. Triggers on "LLM as judge", "grade this", "rubric", "score the answer", "is this output good", "judge". ([Luis247911/universal-ai-workspace-foundation](https://github.com/Luis247911/universal-ai-workspace-foundation/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-judge))
- **eval-judge**: Score an eval run's judge bundles with fresh Claude subagents against the suite rubric, then build the report. Use after `python -m evals.cli run` in Marketing-Automation, or when asked to "judge the eval run", "score the bundles" or "run the Claude judge". ([sontung2310/Marketing-Automation-Platform](https://github.com/sontung2310/Marketing-Automation-Platform/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-judge))
- **eval-jueces**: Mide si los jueces (`code-judge`, `security-judge`) detectan de verdad lo que su rubrica promete. Corre el corpus de fixtures de `.claude/evals/` contra el juez y reporta recall, falsos positivos y precision de criterio. Usar SIEMPRE despues de editar el prompt de un juez o de agregar un criterio a su rubrica, y antes de confiar en un veredicto nuevo. ([Akxlarre/Koa-agent-cli](https://github.com/Akxlarre/Koa-agent-cli/tree/HEAD/templates/.claude/skills/eval-jueces))
- **eval-key-challenge**: Use when a run's answer disagrees with the recorded answer key and you must establish whether the KEY is derivable from this chart, before anyone concludes the run was wrong. Tells you the three ways a disagreement arises, what evidence separates them, the cheap structural check to run first, and why naming a winner is not your job. Does not decide whether the run was correct - that comparison is the deterministic scorer's and is already done. ([YeechingTiger/agentic-chart-review-with-semantica](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/agentic-chart-review-with-semantica/tree/HEAD/assets/skills/eval-key-challenge))
- **eval-kit**: Use when scaffolding or reading claude plugin eval runs: write case.yaml, pilot one run under a cost cap, confirm the plugin loaded, then read the with-minus-without delta. ([DojoGenesis/plugins](https://github.com/DojoGenesis/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/dojo-settle/skills/eval-kit) · ★1)
- **eval-kit**: Decides whether an LLM eval change is real or noise (A/B tests of prompts, models, or agents), using Welch's or paired t-tests, effect sizes, confidence intervals, and power analysis instead of eyeballing means. Covers which test fits the data, how many runs or items to collect before testing (sample size), how to read the verdict, and when the data cannot support a conclusion at all. ([TracineHQ/eval-kit](https://github.com/TracineHQ/eval-kit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **eval-lab**: `eval-lab` est le **gardien de contrat** de l'écosystème TricorderKit. Il valide que : 1. Chaque skill produit un output conforme à `skill_output.schema.json` v1.0.0 2. Aucune régression silencieuse n'est introduite lors d'une modification de skill 3. Les baselines de référence sont persistées et comparées automatiquement ([GeekFamilyCorp/TricorderKit](https://github.com/GeekFamilyCorp/TricorderKit/tree/HEAD/plugins/eval-lab) · ★33)
- **eval-ladder**: Design and audit the eval ladder for an agent system: build tiers bottom-up from observed failures, pick the cheapest rung that can catch a given regression, name what each rung structurally cannot prove, validate every LLM judge against human labels (TPR and TNR separately, never raw agreement), and score irreversible-action scenarios pass^k rather than by majority. Use when designing, auditing, or defending a test/eval strategy for an agent, skill, or prompt; when adding an eval tier or LLM judge; when a suite is all-green and you cannot say what it would catch; or on phrases like "assess o… ([JRichlen/agent-plugins](https://github.com/JRichlen/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/eval-ladder/skills/eval-ladder) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **eval-lane**: Use when running RFP RAG/agent evaluation lanes, checking gate status, rebuilding indexes, comparing eval runs, or verifying gates before a PR. ([hskim-solv/rfp-rag](https://github.com/hskim-solv/rfp-rag/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-lane) · 2 copies)
- **eval-layer**: Generate an evaluation layer from an agent's code, instructions, domain context, tools, and memory, using Jev or an LLM judge. Use to create agent-specific rubrics, test cases, execution checks, and reproducible judge comparisons for existing agents or Codex coding workflows. - **eval-suite**: Run the build, attack, rebuild loop for AI eval suites. - **eval-suite-auditor**: Audits an existing evaluation pipeline (LLM judges, assertions, scorers, repair or regression gates) for silent failure by probing it, not by reading the code. - **eval-suite-planner**: Plan standalone — populates the Eval Suite Planning & Logging Template from an Agent Vision or plain-English agent description. Output is a template-preserving `.xlsx` workbook plus an interactive HTML review page. Use before generating test cases or running evals. - **eval-suite-planner**: Produces a concrete eval suite plan grounded in Microsoft's Eval Scenario Library and MS Learn agent evaluation guidance — scenario types, evaluation methods, quality signals, thresholds, and priority order — before any test cases are generated or evals are run. - **eval-suite-planner**: Produces a concrete eval suite plan for AI agents - grounded in Microsoft's Eval Scenario Library and MS Learn agent evaluation guidance (Copilot Studio is the primary worked example, but the plan is platform-agnostic and adapts to any agent harness). Outputs scenario types, evaluation methods, quality signals, thresholds, and priority order - before any test cases are generated or evals are run. - **eval-suite-run**: Runs the orchestrator's own evaluation suite — 3 to 5 real, already solved tasks replayed headless in throwaway worktrees — and compares pass rate, cost, turns and time with the baseline.
